# Agents · M · part 4

976 entries, memos-logger to migration-actor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **memos-logger**: スクリーンショットや画像を Memos にアップロードして共有する。 画像取得時に呼び出され、画像ファイルパスを受け取って投稿する。 ([koei-kaji/dotfiles](https://github.com/koei-kaji/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memos-logger.md) · ★1)
- **mempalace**: Long-term project memory for `protoArtoo` lives in MemPalace (MCP server plus a user-level daemon), in one wing: **`wing_protoartoo`**. The convention on this machine is `wing_&lt;project&gt;`; the bare `protoartoo` is not a wing, and `protoArtoo` case-sensitively matches nothing and answers "No results found" rather than failing - so a wing-scoped search against it reads as *no prior art* when it means ([mattiasbrandt/protoArtoo](https://github.com/mattiasbrandt/protoArtoo/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/mempalace.md) · ★2)
- **mempenny**: Runs MemPenny memory-cleanup commands. Auto-approves the non-destructive bash and file operations they need (reads, backup, validation, atomic writes, subagent spawns); still asks before any rm, and before any bash command outside mempenny's known-safe set. Use this agent only for mempenny commands — the relaxed permissions are scoped to them. ([marcelopaniza/mempenny](https://github.com/marcelopaniza/mempenny/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/mempenny.md) · ★2)
- **mempool-agent**: Analyze blockchain mempools for MEV opportunities, sandwich attack patterns, pending large transfers, and gas price optimization across Ethereum, BSC, and Arbitrum. Use when detecting front-running risk, profiling pending transactions, or researching block builder behavior. Trigger with "analyze mempool", "detect MEV". ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/crypto/mempool-analyzer/agents/mempool-agent.md) · ★2.8k)
- **mempool-agent**: Mempool analysis specialist for MEV detection and pending transaction monitoring ([jeremylongshore/plugins-nixtla](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/plugins-nixtla/blob/HEAD/010-archive/backups-20251108/plugin-enhancements/plugin-backups/mempool-analyzer_20251020_051129/agents/mempool-agent.md) · ★11 · 9 copies)

- **memsy-archivist**: Deep-retrieval subagent for Memsy. Use when shallow recall (a single memsy_search call) returns thin or off-target results and the user needs a thorough, multi-query exploration of past memory. Iterates search, clusters by theme, refines queries, dedupes, and returns a structured summary instead of a flat list. Invoke this agent for queries like "what's our full thinking on X", "audit our past decisions on Y", "find everything related to Z". ([memsy-io/memsy](https://github.com/memsy-io/memsy/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-code/agents/memsy-archivist.md) · ★32)

- **Metis Arbiter**: L4 terminal leaf - ARBITER. Independent decision-maker for forks the loop cannot resolve on its own. - **Metis Depth-prober**: L4 terminal leaf - G3.5 DEPTH-LOCK. Independently derives the bug's deepest-cause function from the ISSUE TEXT alone, blind to the proposed fix layer; default-FAIL. - **Metis Execharness-resolver**: L3 executor - EXECHARNESS RESOLVE. Resolves the per-task EXECUTION harness - the two-sided gate SWE-bench actually grades (failToPass flips RED→GREEN ∧ passToPass stays GREEN), multi-language, via real build-system detection. Ingests shipped FAIL_TO_PASS/PASS_TO_PASS, else derives failToPass from the mission's behavioral acceptance asks. An unresolvable environment is BLOCKED, never a stand-in. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-execharness-resolver.md) · ★143)

- **Metis Framework-generator**: L3 executor - FRAMEWORK GENERATE. When the SELECTOR returns MISS, generates a one-off custom framework for the exact task shape - classifies the orthogonal axes, composes the gate sequence from the GATE-LIBRARY with the correct axis-specific gate, emits the gen-&lt;axis-signature&gt; leaf with the BLOCKED invariant verbatim, binds an execharness, and hands it to the validator before any gate runs. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-framework-generator.md) · ★143)
- **Metis Framework-validator**: L4 terminal leaf - FRAMEWORK VALIDATE (HRN-5). A fresh, default-FAIL juror that proves a GENERATED framework is SOUND before any gate runs. Checks the HRN-5 default-FAIL checklist - every gate mapped, exactly one terminal DONE with negatives looping UP, the BLOCKED invariant verbatim, a non-empty acceptance set. PASS lets the leaf be driven; FAIL with numbered reasons returns it to the generator. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-framework-validator.md) · ★143)
- **Metis Fresh-verifier**: L4 blind fresh verifier - independently checks a candidate roadmap or plan against the exact mission and repository; APPROVE/REJECT, default-FAIL. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-fresh-verifier.md) · ★143)
- **Metis Goal-checker**: L4 terminal leaf - GOAL-CHECK. Independent, adversarial, default-FAIL. Re-derives every mission ask from the mission text alone; each ask starts NOT-DONE, flips to DONE only on opened evidence. DONE only if zero open findings at ANY severity AND user-usable AND (coverage &gt;=95% only when the admitted oracle is code-behavior TDD) AND a tri-axis end-to-end run (scope + original prompt + potential flaws) is on record. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-goal-checker.md) · ★143)
- **Metis Implementer**: L3 executor - G4 IMPLEMENT. Builds one feature from its approved executable roadmap item or conditional frozen plan using strict TDD and real test runs; coverage &gt;=95% on changed lines when the admitted oracle is code-behavior TDD. Reports PLAN-CONFLICT rather than improvising. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-implementer.md) · ★143)
- **Metis Intake**: L3 legacy-resume compatibility reader - reconstructs old intake artifacts when explicitly resuming them; new runs use the useful-first roadmap author instead. ([Wholiver/metis](https://github.com/Wholiver/metis/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek/agents/metis-intake.md) · ★143)
- **Metis Janitor**: L4 terminal leaf - JANITOR. Writes the DONE sentinel atomically and removes only scratch artifacts after the three-file governance state and substantive evidence pass validation. - **metodo**: Agente del MÉTODO de la rearquitectura por capas (Analisis/41, fase F6): documentación por zona en docs/arquitectura/ con índice de secciones viejas, bitácora por mes y ESTADO.md corto, script de estado generado, CLAUDE.md con solo invariantes, gancho de Claude Code por carpeta, agentes por zona, pruebas puras separadas de las del emulador. Usar cuando haya que mover o mantener documentación, agentes o ganchos sin tocar código de Functions, consola ni flujos. Escribe solo en docs/, bitacora/, .claude/hooks/, .claude/agents/ y CLAUDE.md. ([segurolotengopy/NovuChat](https://github.com/segurolotengopy/NovuChat/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/metodo.md))
- **metodologo**: Metodólogo de pesquisa científica. Use quando o aluno disser "começar minha pesquisa", "definir a pergunta", "qual método", "montar o projeto", "amostra", "instrumento", "análise", "banca", "ABNT", ou fizer qualquer pergunta sobre como desenhar, executar ou escrever uma pesquisa. Conduz o aluno pelo manual MirandasTech de Metodologia, constrói o projeto conforme a NBR 15287:2025 e controla o checklist local (PROGRESSO.md (seção «Método»)). Para qualquer pergunta sobre USO DE IA na pesquisa, encaminha ao agente "orientador". - **migrate-plugin**: Use when the user has an existing COM-pattern Cimatron API plugin (class implementing interop.CimBaseAPI.ICimCommand + ICreateCommand, registered via regsvr32 or COM TLB) and wants to migrate it to the Cimatron 2026 Plugin pattern (class implementing CimUIInfrastructure.PlugIn.ICimApiCommandPlugin, registered via ExternalCommands.ini). Read-only assessment first, then surgical edits with rollback notes. Reverse direction (Plugin → COM) is covered by api-scaffold. ([cimatron-dev/Cimatron-API-Claude-Docs](https://github.com/cimatron-dev/Cimatron-API-Claude-Docs/blob/HEAD/plugins/cimatron-api/agents/migrate-plugin.md) · ★9)
- **migrate-review**: Independent review of a migrated screen's code against the BLoC → utopia_hooks exit gate. Fresh context, does not see the migration agent's reasoning. Runs Phase 3 self-review and Phase 4 exit gate from the skill, returns pass/fail + fix list. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-hooks-migrate-bloc/agents/migrate-review.md) · ★1)
- **migrate-screen**: Migrate a single Flutter screen (plus any global states it needs that aren't yet migrated) from BLoC/Cubit to utopia_hooks. Produces the diff but does not commit. For god-screens it proposes a staged plan executed across multiple invocations. Follows the migrate-bloc-to-utopia-hooks skill strictly. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-hooks-migrate-bloc/agents/migrate-screen.md) · ★1)
